# built at each commit (git worktree, `cargo build --release --bin lexindex`), over
# string-index-benchmarks' corpora at blocks 256 and 1024, each followed by `inspect --sections`.
# Steps: 0d59b31 46061c0 46c9f4e 6fd4019 b1921c8 a03a167 26aac78 30df482 ef8996e 4499e16 5be18d8
# 8a8778a ef47ded; script local/a4a/todo2.sh (untracked), its loop:
#   lexindex build <corpus>.txt out.bdx --index dict --block <b>; stat -c %s; lexindex inspect out.bdx --sections
# Sizes are deterministic. Lines: `rev <commit> <corpus> <block> <bytes>`, then the inspect output.
